     OUR CORE VALUES                                                                                    THE SABIC LEADERSHIP WAY

     Our values serve as the foundation of our organization                                             Our leadership framework is the set of guiding principles
     and culture. They govern our behavior and drive our                                                that describes the unique characteristics for leadership
     commitment to act with the highest ethical standards. We                                           success at SABIC. A leader at SABIC refers to everyone
     have integrated them into the fabric of our organization as                                        from the manufacturing floor to the CEO. Our leaders
     a reference point for our business practice and to ensure                                          are compelled to demonstrate courage and integrity
     successful implementation of our strategy.                                                         by accepting challenges and voicing concerns in a
                                                                                                        positive way. They lead by example and hold each other
                                                                                                        accountable. There are four leadership priorities that, along
                                                                                                        with our values, inform how we lead and behave every day.

                                                                                                                   BOARD MEETINGS
                                                                                                                   The Board of Directors meets at least twice a year
                                                                                                                   at the Chairman's invitation. The Chairman may
                                                                                                                   also convene a meeting if so requested by two
                                                                                                                   members of the Board, the external auditor, the
                                                                                                                   internal audit department, or the CEO. The
                                                                                                                   quorum for a Board meeting is at least five
                                                                                                                   members. A member of the Board has the right to
                                                                                                                   authorize another member to attend the meeting
                                                                                                                   on his behalf.
